- Find relevant pages in index.md by title, aliases (aka) and categories.

(part 87/96)

# Chapters
Aliases: Chapter, Lotus Chapters
[Chapters]
Chapters are groups of progression tasks for the [[Backwater Bayou]], [[Lotus Atoll]], [[Moonglade Marsh]], and [[Torrhus Canyon]] islands. Each island has 7 tiers of chapters, each with multiple tasks that can be completed once to earn rewards. Every task in a chapter tier must be completed to unlock the next tier. Chapters can be accessed by using the [[SkyBlock Menu]], typing /chapters, or speaking to a specific [[NPC]] on its respective island, indicated by the ISLAND GUIDE text.
## List of Chapters
### Backwater Bayou
The [[Backwater Bayou]] has a total of 38 tasks across 7 chapters. Completing all these tasks rewards the [[Ship|Pearl of the Swamp]], which grants a permanent +1 [[Treasure Chance]]. These chapters can be accessed by speaking to [[Jonesy]].
Begin your journey through the Seven Seas.:
|Task|Description|Reward|
|-|-|-|
|Learn About Junk ([[Junk Sinker]])|[[Junker Joel]] will teach you about how to fish Junk ([[Junk Sinker]]).|+100 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
|Apply a Rod Part ([[Rod Parts]])|[[Roddy]] can apply [[Rod Parts]], which can help with customizing your fishing rods.|+100 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
|Learn About Hotspots ([[Hotspot (disambiguation)]])|[[Fishing Hotspots]] grant bonus stats when fishing in them, and allow you to catch Hotspot Sea Creatures ([[Sea Creatures]]). They can be found around many islands.|+100 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|

[Unlock Chapter II; 4 Treasure Bait; +5 SkyBlock XP (SkyBlock Levels)]

|Catch a [[Dumpster Diver]]|This is a UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]]) Bayou Sea Creature ([[Sea Creatures]]) found on the [[Backwater Bayou]]. It's a little bit more rare than the [[Trash Gobbler]].|+250 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|

### Lotus Atoll
The [[Lotus Atoll]] has a total of 47 tasks across 7 chapters. Completing all these tasks rewards the [[Ship|Pearl of the Lotus]], which grants a permanent +2.5 [[Trophy Chance]]. These chapters can be accessed by speaking to [[Researcher Betty]].
Begin your journey as Batrachologist.:
|Task|Description|Reward|
|-|-|-|
|Talk to [[Researcher Ribery]]|[[Researcher Ribery]] is the head of the whole operation. View his Research Journal to see all species of Trophy Frog ([[Trophy Frogs]]) you can catch!|+250 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
|Catch a BRONZE Trophy Frog ([[Trophy Frogs]])|[[Trophy Frogs]] can be exchanged with [[Researcher Ribery]] in return for [[Lotus]].|+250 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
|Catch an [[Atoll Croaker]]|This is a common Lotus Sea Creature ([[Sea Creatures]]) found on the Lotus Atoll.|+250 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|

[Unlock Chapter II; 40 Lotus; +5 SkyBlock XP (SkyBlock Levels)]

### Moonglade Marsh
The [[Moonglade Marsh]] has a total of 69 tasks across 7 chapters. These chapters can be accessed by speaking to [[Hina]] in [[Tangleburg's Path]].
Begin your journey through the forest.:
|Task|Description|Reward|
|-|-|-|
|Talk to [[Swoop]]|[[Swoop]] will teach you the basics of [[Foraging]] on [[Galatea]].|+100 Foraging XP ([[Foraging]])|
|Use the Fusion Machine ([[Attribute Fusion]])|The Fusion Machine ([[Attribute Fusion]]) will be extremely useful in your [[Hunting]] journey.|+100 Hunting XP ([[Hunting]])|
|Talk to David ([[David Hunterborough]]) and his Hu ([[Oden]])nt ([[Jaeger]])ers ([[Auryon]])|David ([[David Hunterborough]])'s quest teaches you the ropes of [[Hunting]].|+100 Hunting XP ([[Hunting]])|

### Torrhus Canyon
The [[Torrhus Canyon]] has a total of 65 tasks across 7 chapters. These chapters can be accessed by speaking to [[Helia]] in [[Torrhus Canyon]].
Begin your journey through Torrhus Canyon.:
|Task|Description|Reward|
|-|-|-|
|Talk to [[Miria]]|Miria is one of the Starlyn Sisters. She holds Miria's Contests across Torrhus Canyon each day.|+250 [[Desert Whispers]]|
|Open a Helix Tree ([[Helix Log]]) Gift|Helix Trees are native to Torrhus Canyon. They have higher Toughness than Fig and Mangrove Trees and require you to entirely cut the Beige twin helix before moving on to the Red one to avoid a Sweep Penalty!|64 (1 stack) [[Helix Log]]|
|Loot a Honeyhive ([[Honeyhives]])|Honeyhives can be found around Torrhus Canyon - there's even one right by the entrance of the island! You can reach into them in search of Loot, such as Shards and Honeycomb. They refill after a while and are the main source of the Honeycomb Collection.|16 [[Honeycomb]]|
[Chapter I Completion Rewards]
[Unlock Chapter II; 1 Honeybuzz Shard; +250 Heart Of The Forest XP (Heart of the Forest); +2,500 Foraging XP (Foraging); +5 SkyBlock XP (SkyBlock Levels)]
Learn the way around and complete your first Hunts.:
|Task|Description|Reward|
|-|-|-|
|Obtain 10 Helix ([[Helix Log]]) [[Tree Gifts]]|Having more [[Sweep]] will let you cut down these hardy trees faster! Remember to entirely cut the Beige twin helix before moving on to the Red one to avoid a Sweep Penalty!|1 [[Enchanted Helix Log]]|
|Participate in Miria's Contest ([[Starlyn Contest]])|Miria will reward for participating in her daily Starlyn Contests while on Torrhus Canyon!|+500 [[Desert Whispers]]|
|Hunt a [[Bunbun]]|Bunbuns hop freely around Torrhus Canyon. You can hunt them using a Lasso.|+500 Hunting XP ([[Hunting]])|
|Hunt a [[Firefox]]|Firefoxes frequently spawn from Helix Tree Gifts. You can hunt them using a Lasso.|+1,000 Hunting XP ([[Hunting]])|
|Hunt a [[Groundhog]]|Groundhogs frequently spawn from Helix Tree Gifts. You can hunt them using a Lasso.|+1,000 Hunting XP ([[Hunting]])|
|Purchase a Hint from [[Freddy]]|Freddy knows where just about every Critter ([[Critters]]) that is native to Torrhus Canyon can be found. He will share his wisdom with you - for a price.|+1,000 [[Desert Whispers]]|
[Chapter II Completion Rewards]
[Unlock Chapter III; 1x Bunbun Shard 1x Firefox Shard 1x Groundhog Shard +500 Heart Of The Forest XP (Heart of the Forest); +1,000 Desert Whispers (Desert Whispers); +5,000 Foraging XP (Foraging); +10 SkyBlock XP (SkyBlock Levels)]
Take your first steps into the Critter Safari!:
|Task|Description|Reward|
|-|-|-|

Remember to entirely cut the Beige twin helix before moving on to the Red one to avoid a Sweep Penalty!|2 [[Enchanted Helix Log]]|
|Reach UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]]) in Miria's Contest|Finishing in the UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]]) bracket will give you a Basic Safari Ticket, which can be used to enter the Critter Safari!|1 Basic Safari Ticket ([[Safari Tickets]])|
|Hunt a [[Drybark]]|Drybarks occasionally spawn from Helix Tree Gifts. You can hunt them using a Lasso.|+2,500 Hunting XP ([[Hunting]])|
|Hunt a [[Puck]]|Pucks occasionally spawn from Helix Tree Gifts. You can hunt them using a Lasso.|+2,500 Hunting XP ([[Hunting]])|
|Pick up a Floor Drop ([[Floor Drops]])|You can find a whole new set of Floor Drops in Torrhus Canyon, including Dung Beetle Shards.|+2,500 [[Desert Whispers]]|
|Craft a [[Fun-Sized Pot of Honeycomb]]|The Fun-Sized Pot of Honeycomb unlocks at Honeycomb Collection I. Smear it on a Tree, wait a while, and a rare Critter ([[Critters]]) will show up! |Obtain a [[Safari Belt]] from [[Andrea]]|Andrea works at the Critter Safari Entrance. She'll give you your very own Safari Belt, which upgrades as you progress through her Safari Milestones.|10 [[Safari Essence]]|

|Catch 5 Unique [[Critters]] in the [[Critter Safari]]|The Critter Safari is comprised of 4 unique biomes, each with its own set of Critter ([[Critters]]) for you to Hunt!|10 [[Safari Essence]]|

|Hunt a [[Dustybit]]|Dustybits live around the Spring Path. Chase them down to get their Shard!|+2,500 Hunting XP ([[Hunting]])|

|Hunt a [[Goldolot]]|Goldolots ([[Goldolot]]) are a rare mutation of a [[Sepialot]]. Sneak up on them with a Fishing Net ([[Fishing Nets]]) to Hunt them!|+5,000 Hunting XP ([[Hunting]])|

# Chapters/Backwater Bayou
Begin your journey through the Seven Seas.:
|Task|Description|Reward|
|-|-|-|
|Learn About Junk ([[Junk Sinker]])|[[Junker Joel]] will teach you about how to fish Junk ([[Junk Sinker]]).|+100 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
|Apply a Rod Part ([[Rod Parts]])|[[Roddy]] can apply [[Rod Parts]], which can help with customizing your fishing rods.|+100 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
|Learn About Hotspots ([[Hotspot (disambiguation)]])|[[Fishing Hotspots]] grant bonus stats when fishing in them, and allow you to catch Hotspot Sea Creatures ([[Sea Creatures]]). They can be found around many islands.|+100 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
[Chapter I Completion Rewards]
[Unlock Chapter II; 4 Treasure Bait; +5 SkyBlock XP (SkyBlock Levels)]
Grab your rod and join the cleanup effort! Let's catch some Junk!:
|Task|Description|Reward|
|-|-|-|
|Reach [[Lily Pad]] Collection I|Bayou Sea Creatures ([[Sea Creatures]]) drop Lily Pads ([[Lily Pad]]) when killed. Progress through your [[Lily Pad]] Collection to unlock new crafting recipes!|+250 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
|Catch a [[Rusty Coin]]|When using a [[Junk Sinker]], a majority of your Junk ([[Junk Sinker]]) catches will be a [[Rusty Coin]].|+250 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
|Purchase [[Treasure Bait]]|[[Treasure Bait]] can be purchased from [[Junker Joel]]. It increases your chance of catching [[Treasure]] and Junk ([[Junk Sinker]]).|+250 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
|Catch a [[Trash Gobbler]]|This is a COMMON ([[Rarity]]) Bayou Sea Creature ([[Sea Creatures]]) found on the [[Backwater Bayou]].|+250 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
|Catch a [[Dumpster Diver]]|This is a UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]]) Bayou Sea Creature ([[Sea Creatures]]) found on the [[Backwater Bayou]]. It's a little bit more rare than the [[Trash Gobbler]].|+250 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
[Chapter II Completion Rewards]
[Unlock Chapter III; 1x Rusty Coin 8x Treasure Bait; +10 SkyBlock XP (SkyBlock Levels)]
Start trading in Junk at Junker Joel in exchange for some powerful upgrades to your gear..:
|Task|Description|Reward|
|-|-|-|
|Reach [[Lily Pad]] Collection III|Bayou Sea Creatures ([[Sea Creatures]]) drop Lily Pads ([[Lily Pad]]) when killed. Progress through your [[Lily Pad]] Collection to unlock new crafting recipes!|+500 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
|Purchase a [[Junk Talisman]]|The [[Junk Talisman]] increases your [[Treasure Chance]] while in the [[Backwater Bayou]]! Put it in your [[Accessory Bag]] to gain its effects.|+500 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
|Purchase a [[Challenging Rod]]|Your Fishing Rod ([[Fishing Rods]]) will only get your so far - purchase an upgrade from [[Junker Joel]] today!|+500 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
|Purchase a Piece of [[Backwater Armor]]|Start upgrading the [[Angler Armor]] you purchased at the [[Fishing Outpost]] into [[Backwater Armor]]!|+500 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
|Purchase a [[Hermit Crab Pet]]|Pets start at COMMON ([[Rarity]]) rarity, but you can talk to [[Kat]] in the Hub ([[Hub Island]]) to upgrade their rarity, which improves their existing perks and unlocks new ones!|+500 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
|Catch a Hotspot Sea Creature ([[Sea Creatures]])|Hotspots ([[Hotspot (disambiguation)]]) grant stat bonuses when fishing in them, and allow you to catch Hotspot Sea Creatures ([[Sea Creatures]]).|+500 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
[Chapter III Completion Rewards]
[Unlock Chapter IV; 4x Rusty Coin 16x Treasure Bait; +15 SkyBlock XP (SkyBlock Levels)]
You're a true resident of the Backwater Bayou now, but stronger challenges await.:
|Task|Description|Reward|
|-|-|-|
|Reach [[Lily Pad]] Collection V|Bayou Sea Creatures ([[Sea Creatures]]) drop Lily Pads ([[Lily Pad]]) when killed. Progress through your [[Lily Pad]] Collection to unlock new crafting recipes!|+1,000 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
|Catch a [[Busted Belt Buckle]]|Sometimes your Junk ([[Junk Sinker]]) catches will be a [[Busted Belt Buckle]].|+1,000 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
|Catch a [[Banshee]]|This is a RARE ([[Rarity]]) Bayou Sea Creature ([[Sea Creatures]]) found on the [[Backwater Bayou]].|+1,000 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
|Upgrade [[Hermit Crab Pet]] to UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]]) at [[Kat]]|You can upgrade your [[Hermit Crab Pet]] by talking to Kat in the Hub ([[Hub Island]]).|+1,000 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
|Apply a [[Stingy Sinker]] to Your Rod|When applied to your rod, the [[Stingy Sinker]] will sometimes save [[Bait]] from being consumed.|+1,000 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
|Purchase a [[Junk Ring]]|The [[Junk Talisman]] is good, but the [[Junk Ring]] is better. Upgrade today at [[Junker Joel]]!|+1,000 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
[Chapter IV Completion Rewards]
[Unlock Chapter V; 1x Busted Belt Buckle 8x Rusty Coin 32x Treasure Bait; +20 SkyBlock XP (SkyBlock Levels)]
Time to upgrade the biggest hunk of junk of them all - your Ship!:
|Task|Description|Reward|
|-|-|-|
|Reach [[Lily Pad]] Collection VII|Bayou Sea Creatures ([[Sea Creatures]]) drop Lily Pads ([[Lily Pad]]) when killed. Progress through your [[Lily Pad]] Collection to unlock new crafting recipes!|+2,500 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
|Catch a [[Bayou Sludge]]|This is a EPIC ([[Rarity]]) Sea Creature ([[Sea Creatures]]) found on the [[Backwater Bayou]].|+2,500 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
|Apply a [[Bronze Ship Helm]]|This Ship upgrade is crafted with Bronze Bowls ([[Bronze Bowl]]), which drop rarely from Dumpster Divers ([[Dumpster Diver]]).|+2,500 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
|Apply a [[Bronze Ship Engine]]|Purchasing this Ship upgrade from [[Junker Joel]] and applying it to your Ship will allow you to go on a new adventure to further away Fishing Islands.|+2,500 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
|Upgrade [[Hermit Crab Pet]] to RARE ([[Rarity]]) at [[Kat]]|You can upgrade your [[Hermit Crab Pet]] by talking to Kat in the Hub ([[Hub Island]]).|+2,500 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
|Apply a [[Speedy Line]] to Your Rod|The [[Speedy Line]] grants [[Fishing Speed]], which increases how quickly you get a bite when fishing. It's useful no matter what you're trying to catch!|+2,500 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|

[Unlock Chapter VI 2x Busted Belt Buckle 16x Rusty Coin 64x Treasure Bait; +25 SkyBlock XP (SkyBlock Levels)]
Some Junk is pretty rare, but you'll need it if you want to get the strongest upgrades.:
|Task|Description|Reward|
|-|-|-|

|Catch an [[Alligator]]|This is a LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]]) Sea Creature ([[Sea Creatures]]) found on the [[Backwater Bayou]].|+5,000 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|

|Upgrade [[Hermit Crab Pet]] to EPIC ([[Rarity]]) at [[Kat]]|You can upgrade your [[Hermit Crab Pet]] by talking to Kat in the Hub ([[Hub Island]]).|+5,000 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
|Purchase a [[Junk Artifact]]|The [[Junk Ring]] is better, but the [[Junk Artifact]] is best. Upgrade today at [[Junker Joel]]!|+5,000 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|

Finish up with the Backwater Bayou and claim the Pearl of the Swamp!:
|Task|Description|Reward|
|-|-|-|
|Reach [[Lily Pad]] Collection IX|Bayou Sea Creatures ([[Sea Creatures]]) drop Lily Pads ([[Lily Pad]]) when killed. Progress through your [[Lily Pad]] Collection to unlock new crafting recipes!|+10,000 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
|Catch a [[Titanoboa]]|This MYTHIC ([[Rarity]]) Sea Creature ([[Sea Creatures]]) is the rarest and most dangerous creature in the [[Backwater Bayou]].|+10,000 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
|Craft a [[Bayou Water Orb]]|The [[Bayou Water Orb]] is needed in some high-level crafts and purchases.|+10,000 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|

# Chapters/Lotus Atoll
Begin your journey as Batrachologist.:
|Task|Description|Reward|
|-|-|-|
|Talk to [[Researcher Ribery]]|[[Researcher Ribery]] is the head of the whole operation. View his Research Journal to see all species of Trophy Frog ([[Trophy Frogs]]) you can catch!|+250 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
|Catch a BRONZE Trophy Frog ([[Trophy Frogs]])|[[Trophy Frogs]] can be exchanged with [[Researcher Ribery]] in return for [[Lotus]].|+250 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
|Catch an [[Atoll Croaker]]|This is a common Lotus Sea Creature ([[Sea Creatures]]) found on the Lotus Atoll.|+250 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
[Chapter I Competition Rewards]
[Unlock Chapter II; 40 Lotus; +5 SkyBlock XP (SkyBlock Levels)]
Get to know the Lotus Atoll a little better.:
|Task|Description|Reward|
|-|-|-|
|Talk to [[Researcher Lily]]|[[Researcher Lily]] is investigating a strange phenomenon on the island, and knows how to catch a rare species of Trophy Frog ([[Trophy Frogs]]) known as the Reality Hopper ([[Trophy Frogs]]).|+500 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
|Talk to [[Researcher Tina]]|As per [[Researcher Tina]]'s findings, the [[Trophy Chance]] stat increases your chance of catching Trophy Frog ([[Trophy Frogs]]).|+500 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
|Catch a SILVER Trophy Frog ([[Trophy Frogs]])|[[Trophy Frogs]] can be exchanged with [[Researcher Ribery]] in return for [[Lotus]].|+500 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
|Catch a Bullfrog ([[Trophy Frogs]])|Bullfrogs ([[Trophy Frogs]]) will only bite if you're wearing a [[Red Sweater]].|+500 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
|Hunt a [[Lotusfish]]|[[Lotusfish]] can be hunted using a Fishing Net ([[Fishing Nets]]).; You can learn more about Attribute Shards ([[Attributes]]) by talking to [[David Hunterborough]] over in the [[Moonglade Marsh]].|+500 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
|Hunt a [[Lotum]]|Lotums ([[Lotum]]) like to jump between lotus. They'll drop a [[Lotum Shard]] if you manage to chase them down.; You can learn more about Attribute Shards ([[Attributes]]) by talking to [[David Hunterborough]] over in the [[Moonglade Marsh]].|+500 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
|Catch a [[Lotus Guardian]]|This is an UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]]) Lotus Sea Creature ([[Sea Creatures]]) found on the [[Lotus Atoll]].|+500 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
|Catch a [[GorF]]|This is a RARE ([[Rarity]]) Lotus Sea Creature ([[Sea Creatures]]) found on the [[Lotus Atoll]].|+500 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
|Craft [[Froggles]]|[[Froggles]] can be crafted after reaching [[Lotus]] Collection I. They allow you to see Wormholes ([[Wormhole]])!|+500 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
|Reach [[Lotus]] Collection II|Progress through your [[Lotus]] Collection by exchanging [[Trophy Frogs]] with [[Researcher Ribery]]!|+500 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
[Chapter II Competition Rewards]
[Unlock Chapter III; 1 Travel Scroll to the Lotus Atoll; 5 Silver Lotus; +10 SkyBlock XP (SkyBlock Levels)]
Keep progressing through your Lotus Collection and catching new types of Trophy Frogs.:
|Task|Description|Reward|
|-|-|-|
|Talk to [[Researcher Kay]]|[[Researcher Kay]] is a bit of an oddball, and prefers to do her own thing.|+1,000 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
|Craft [[Silver Froggles]]|[[Silver Froggles]] are a direct upgrade to [[Froggles]] and grant better stats. Crafting them requires [[Lotus]] Collection IV.|+1,000 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
|Catch a Reality Hopper ([[Trophy Frogs]])|If you need help finding one, [[Researcher Lily]] is an expert on the matter!|+1,000 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
|Explode a Lily Pad|You can push Lily Pads together to combine them while on the [[Lotus Atoll]]. When they get large enough, they'll EXPLODE, giving you an Exploding Trophy Frog ([[Trophy Frogs]]) and some other Rewards!|+1,000 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
|Catch a [[Drowned Captain]]|This is an EPIC ([[Rarity]]) Lotus Sea Creature ([[Sea Creatures]]) found on the [[Lotus Atoll]].|+1,000 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
|Hunt a [[Tewtil]]|Tewtils ([[Tewtil]]) can be fed until the explode to obtain a [[Tewtil Shard]].; You can learn more about Attribute Shards ([[Attributes]]) by talking to [[David Hunterborough]] over in the [[Moonglade Marsh]].|+1,000 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
|Reach [[Lotus]] Collection IV|Progress through your [[Lotus]] Collection by exchanging [[Trophy Frogs]] with [[Researcher Ribery]]!|+1,000 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
[Chapter III Competition Rewards]
[Unlock Chapter IV; 10 Silver Lotus; +15 SkyBlock XP (SkyBlock Levels)]
Complete your collection of BRONZE Trophy Frogs.:
|Task|Description|Reward|
|-|-|-|
|Talk to [[Researcher Timmy]]|[[Researcher Timmy]] has noticed something interesting about the weather patterns of the [[Lotus Atoll]].|+2,500 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
|Catch a Wetlands Frog ([[Trophy Frogs]])|Wetlands Frogs ([[Trophy Frogs]]) can only be found while it's Raining.; [[Researcher Timmy]] can tell you when it will Rain next.|+2,500 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
|Catch a Sea Frog ([[Trophy Frogs]])|Sea Frogs ([[Trophy Frogs]]) can only be found underwater.|+2,500 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
|Catch a Tree Frog ([[Trophy Frogs]])|Tree Frogs ([[Trophy Frogs]]) are caught while standing on leaves.|+2,500 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
|Catch a Cave Frog ([[Trophy Frogs]])|Cave Frogs ([[Trophy Frogs]]) can only be caught in the [[Lotus Eater's Cave]].|+2,500 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|

|Hunt a [[Flipflopper]]|Flipfloppers ([[Flipflopper]]) sometimes spawn from Exploding Lily Pad ([[Lotus Atoll]]), and can be hunted using a Fishing Net ([[Fishing Nets]]).; You can obtain your first Fishing Net ([[Fishing Nets]]) by talking to [[David Hunterborough]] in the [[Moonglade Marsh]].|+2,500 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
|Catch all 12 unique BRONZE [[Trophy Frogs]]|Remember to claim your rewards from [[Researcher Ribery]]!|+2,500 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
|Catch a [[Puddle Jumper]]|This is a LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]]) Lotus Sea Creature ([[Sea Creatures]]) found on the [[Lotus Atoll]]. They require a bit more effort than usual to catch.|+2,500 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
|Donate a [[Frogcoin]]|Legends say that those generous enough to make a donation into the Waters of the [[Lotus Eater's Cave]] will have their fortunes blessed.; You can catch Blessed Frogs ([[Trophy Frogs]]) while you have an active Blessing.|+2,500 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
|Reach [[Lotus]] Collection VI|Progress through your [[Lotus]] Collection by exchanging [[Trophy Frogs]] with [[Researcher Ribery]]!|+2,500 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|

# Chapters/Torrhus Canyon
Begin your journey through Torrhus Canyon.:

|Open a Helix Tree ([[Helix Log]]) Gift|Helix Trees are native to Torrhus Canyon. They have higher Toughness than Fig and Mangrove Trees and require you to entirely cut the Beige twin helix before moving on to the Red one to avoid a Sweep Penalty!|64 (1 stack) [[Helix Log]]|
|Loot a Honeyhive ([[Honeyhives]])|Honeyhives can be found around Torrhus Canyon - there's even one right by the entrance of the island! You can reach into them in search of Loot, such as Shards and Honeycomb. They refill after a while and are the main source of the Honeycomb Collection.|16 [[Honeycomb]]|

[Unlock Chapter II; 1 Honeybuzz Shard; +250 Heart Of The Forest XP (Heart of the Forest); +2,500 Foraging XP (Foraging); +5 SkyBlock XP (SkyBlock Levels)]

# Chisels/Gemstones
Each Gemstone type grants a different buff to the Chisel, and is not affected by rarity, unlike all other instances of Gemstone being applied:
|Gemstone Slot|Gemstone Name|Bonus|Value by gemstone tier Rough|Value by gemstone tier Flawed|Value by gemstone tier Fine|Value by gemstone tier Flawless|Value by gemstone tier Perfect|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Gemstone]]|Aquamarine|#% chance to gain an additional Chisel ([[Chisels]]) charge upon starting the Fossil Excavator.|30%|40%|50%|60%|100%|
|[[Gemstone]]|Citrine|#% chance to highlight a piece of treasure upon starting the Fossil Excavator.|30%|40%|50%|60%|100%|
|[[Gemstone]]|Onyx|#% chance to spawn an additional piece of treasure upon starting the Fossil Excavator.|30%|40%|50%|60%|100%|
|[[Gemstone]]|Peridot|Gain +#% Fossil Essence when using the the Fossil Excavator.|30%|40%|50%|60%|100%|

# City Projects/Bartender's Brewery
Aliases: City Project/Bartender's Brewery
Upgrade the [[Bartender]]'s brewery, offering new drinks and a new tier of [[Zombie]] [[Zombie Slayer|Slayer]].
### Contributions
Requires a total of 1,000,000 contributions.
|Title|Materials Required|Fame ([[Fame Ranks]]) Reward|
|-|-|-|
|Building Machinery|2x Enchanted Birch Wood ([[Enchanted Birch Log]]) 1x [[Enchanted Iron Ingot]]|200|
|Sugary drinks|32x [[Enchanted Sugar]] 8x [[Magical Water Bucket]]|200|
|Slavic Recipes|64x [[Enchanted Potato]] 8x [[Magical Water Bucket]]|200|
|Labor|4x [[Enchanted Tropical Fish]] 64x [[Enchanted Melon Slice]] 100 [[Bits]]|600|
### Bonuses
|Contributions|Bonus|
|-|-|
|4|5% discount at [[Bartender]]|
|8|10% discount at [[Bartender]]|
|16|15% discount at [[Bartender]]|
|24|5% discount at [[Maddox the Slayer]]|
In Version 0.27 these bonuses were replaced by [[Attributes|Slayer Discount]] and [[Attributes|Bartender Discount]] [[Attributes]]. They can be reclaimed in the [[Elizabeth]]'s Previous City Projects menu, if the player previously contributed to the project.
Linked from: [[Changelog/2021/March 12]], [[City Projects]], [[Crystal Hollows/Special Locations]], [[God Potion]], [[God Potion (Legacy)]], [[Maddox the Slayer]]

# City Projects/Blacksmith Workspace
Aliases: City Project/Blacksmith Workspace
Add 3 reforges to the [[Blacksmith]] and make his workspace more comfortable.
### Contributions
Requires a total of 500,000 contributions.
|Title|Materials Required|Fame ([[Fame Ranks]]) Reward|
|-|-|-|
|Renovations|64x Spruce Wood ([[Spruce Log]]) 128x (2 stacks (128 items total)) [[Sand]]|200|
|New Anvils|32x [[Enchanted Iron Ingot]] 1x [[Grand Experience Bottle]]|200|
|Fuel for Years|1x [[Enchanted Coal Block]] 1x [[Catalyst]]|200|
|Labor|64x [[Enchanted Raw Beef]] 2x [[Enchanted Melon]] 100 [[Bits]]|600|
### Bonuses
|Contributions|Bonus|
|-|-|
|1|1 day headstart|
|2|3 days headstart|
|3|5 days headstart|
|4|1 week headstart|
Linked from: [[Changelog/2020/October 20]], [[City Projects]]

# City Projects/Builder's House
Aliases: City Project/Builder's House
The Builder shop NPC will move from a stall to its own house, with tons of new blocks for sale without a daily limit.
### Contributions
Requires a total of 500,000 contributions.
|Title|Materials Required|Fame ([[Fame Ranks]]) Reward|
|-|-|-|
|Plenty of Cobble'|512x (8 stacks (512 items total)) [[Cobblestone]]|200|
|Dyes|1x [[Enchanted Ink Sac]] 32x [[Enchanted Cactus Green]] 32x [[Enchanted Lapis Lazuli]]|200|
|New Building|64x Enchanted Oak Wood ([[Enchanted Oak Log]]) 1x [[Enchanted Quartz Block]]|200|
|Labor|64x [[Enchanted Pumpkin]] 2x [[Enchanted Melon]] 100 [[Bits]]|600|
### Bonuses
|Contributions|Bonus|
|-|-|
|1|5% discount at [[Builder]]|
|2|10% discount at [[Builder]]|
|3|15% discount at [[Builder]]|
|4|30% discount at [[Builder]]|
In Version 0.27 these bonuses were replaced by [[Attributes|Builder Discount]] [[Attributes|Attribute]]. It can be reclaimed in the [[Elizabeth]]'s Previous City Projects menu, if the player previously contributed to the project.
Linked from: [[City Projects]]

# City Projects/Farm Merchant's Dwelling
Aliases: City Project/Farm Merchant's Dwelling
Upgrade the [[Farm Merchant]]'s dwelling, adding new tools both to build farms and harvest them quicker!
### Contributions
Requires a total of 400,000 contributions.
|Title|Materials Required|Fame ([[Fame Ranks]]) Reward|
|-|-|-|
|Building Foundations|2x Enchanted Jungle Wood ([[Enchanted Jungle Log]]) 200x (3 stacks plus 8 (200 in total)) [[Dirt]] 64x [[Hay Bale]]|200|
|Share of Harvests|1x [[Enchanted Hay Bale]] 64x [[Enchanted Seeds]]|200|
|Towering Cacti|1x [[Enchanted Cactus]]|200|
|Labor|8x [[Enchanted Raw Mutton]] 64x [[Enchanted Melon Slice]] 100 [[Bits]]|600|
### Bonuses
|Contributions|Bonus|
|-|-|
|4|1 day headstart|
|8|3 days headstart|
|16|5% discount at [[Farm Merchant]] and [[Anita]]|
|24|10% discount at [[Farm Merchant]] and [[Anita]]|
Linked from: [[City Projects]], [[Farming]]

# Composter/Organic Matter Calculator
This page calculates the best items to use for Organic Matter and Machine Fuel based on data retrieved through {{BazaarData}}. For Organic Matter, items worth less than 32 or more than 40,000 Organic Matter are excluded.
## Transclusion
This page can be transcluded to make use of the following variables.
- {{#var:omcalc_best_item}} (currently Enchanted Seeds) is the name of the item with the lowest cost per Organic Matter.
- {{#var:omcalc_organic_matter_value}} (currently 160) is the amount of Organic Matter that the item is worth.
- {{#var:omcalc_cost_per_compost}} (currently 11837.5) is the cost (in coins) to instant buy 4000 Organic Matter worth of the item from the Bazaar.
- {{#var:fuelcalc_best_item}} (currently Oil Barrel) is the name of the fuel item with the lowest cost per Machine Fuel.
- {{#var:fuelcalc_best_item_plural_suffix}} (currently s) is the suffix to be appended to the item name to make it plural.
- {{#var:fuelcalc_fuel_value}} (currently 10000) is the amount of Machine Fuel that the item is worth.
- {{#var:fuelcalc_cost_per_compost}} (currently 692.74) is the cost (in coins) to buy 2000 Machine Fuel worth of the item.
  - Voltas and Oil Barrels are assumed to be bought from the Bazaar with instant buy, and Biofuel has a fixed price from SkyMart.
## Example
The best [[Bazaar]] item for [[Composter|Organic Matter]] is currently [[Enchanted Seeds]] at 11,838 [[Coins]] per 4000 Organic Matter. The best item for [[Machine Fuel]] is currently [[Oil Barrel|Oil Barrels]] at 693 [[Coins]] per 2000 Machine Fuel when purchased from the Bazaar.
For a [[Composter]] that doesn't have any [[Composter|Composter Upgrades]] yet, each [[Compost]] would need 25 [[Enchanted Seeds]] and 0.2 [[Oil Barrel]], which would cost 12,531 [[Coins]]. The Composter would be able to produce 6 Compost each hour, consuming 24,000 Organic Matter and 12,000 Machine Fuel in the process at a cost of 75,182 [[Coins]]. Since each Compost can be sold to the Bazaar at an instant sell price of 12,974.8 [[Coins]], the hourly profit of running a base-level Composter using [[Enchanted Seeds]] and [[Oil Barrel|Oil Barrels]] is 2,667 [[Coins]] per hour before tax. In total, the Composter could run unattended for 100 minutes before running out of Organic Matter, making 10 Compost in that time at a total cost of 125,303 [[Coins]]. The profit for the full 100 minutes would be 4,445 [[Coins]] before tax.
For a Composter with all Composter Upgrades maxed out, each Compost would need on average 1714.3 Organic Matter and 857.1 Machine Fuel, or 10.71 [[Enchanted Seeds]] and 0.086 [[Oil Barrel]], which together would cost 5,371 [[Coins]]. The Composter would be able to produce an average of 63 Compost per hour, consuming 108,000 Organic Matter and 54,000 Machine Fuel in the process at a cost of 75,182 [[Coins]]. The average hourly profit of running a maxed-out Composter using [[Enchanted Seeds]] and [[Oil Barrel|Oil Barrels]] is 479,095 [[Coins]] per hour before tax. In total, the Composter could run unattended for exactly 5 hours before running out of Organic Matter, making an average of 315 Compost in that time at a total cost of 1,691,583 [[Coins]]. The average profit when running the maxed-out Composter for the full 5 hours would be 2,395,479 [[Coins]] before tax.
Keeping a maxed-out Composter topped off continuously for 30 real-life days would result in an expected profit of 344,949,062 [[Coins]] before tax.

# Crimson Isle Main Questline
Aliases: Find the Dean, Find the dean, Lost Research, Lost research
## Universal
The questline begins at the spawn of the [[Crimson Isle]] where [[Elle]] is being attacked by 3 [[Blaze|Blazes]] which the player must kill. Elle will then ask the player to bring her:
- 4 [[Magma Chunk]] - Dropped from [[Magma Cube|Magma Cubes]] in the [[Crimson Fields]]
- 3 [[Blaze Ashes]] - Dropped from regular [[Blaze|Blazes]] in multiple rooms within the [[Stronghold]]
- 2 [[Spectre Dust]] - Dropped from [[Wither Spectre|Wither Spectres]] in the rooms to the left of [[Elle]]
Upon delivering these items to [[Elle]], she will tell the player to explore the island, before running away. In order to trigger the next step in the quest, the player must go to the upper floor of either [[Factions|Faction]]'s community center. Elle will stop the player, insisting they talk to any 4 villagers from each faction with green particles above their head. Finally, Elle will give the player the choice of which faction they would like to join. From here, the questline for both factions differ, although they closely parallel.
## Mages
Upon choosing the Mage faction, Elle will give the player a [[Maddox Batphone]]. Presenting the Batphone to the [[Dean]] in their office ( [[Coordinates|-16, 123, -882]]) will give the player a [[Letter of Recommendation]] which allows access to the second floor of the community center, where [[Queen Nyx]] will formally invite the player to the Mage Faction.
The player's next quest is to buy an [[Abiphones|Abiphone]] from [[Udel]] or [[Nuvian]], and use it to call the Dean. The player must then talk to Elle at the [[Crimson Isle]] spawn, and then follow her to the cliff above [[The Bastion]], where she thinks the stolen research is. If the player arrives to [[Coordinates|-612, 154, -893]] before Elle, she will teleport to meet them there. The player must start at the mouth of the skull and navigate through guards to find the stolen research without being seen by the guards, or walking too close and being heard. The stolen research is located at [[Coordinates|-684, 124, -1048]], and if the player is quick they can run there before Elle finishes her dialogue and the guards spawn.
Elle will then call the player, and then the player must call the [[Dean]], meet them in the [[Mage Council]] in the [[Community Center (Scarleton)|Community Center]]. A conversation will be held there, granting [[Factions|+100 ቾ Mage Reputation]]. Afterwards, the player meets Dean back in their office, where they will ask the player to find their apprentice in the [[Burning Desert]]. Instead of finding their apprentice the player has to talk to [[Captain Ahone]] at [[Coordinates|-526, 86, -885]] who will tell you to keep looking. Then, the apprentice can be found unconscious at [[Coordinates|-521, 104, -916]], where you must call Captain Ahone with an Abiphone, and wait for them to arrive.
The next quest is to bring 10 [[Magma Chunk]] from Magma Cubes, and 5 [[Burning Eye|Burning Eyes]] from [[Flaming Spider|Flaming Spiders]] to the Dean in their office. Completing this will grant [[Factions|+150 ቾ Mage Reputation]].
The Dean will tell the player to talk to [[Igrupan]] [[Coordinates|-31, 93, -824]] who asks the player to help him get his [[Igrupan's Chickens|chickens]] back in the chicken pen by punching or luring them with [[Seeds]]. This will grant [[Factions|+50 ቾ Mage Reputation]].
The player must also talk to [[Odexar]] [[Coordinates|-112, 99, -844]] who gives the player a [[Trophy Fish|Volcanic Stonefish]] to bring to [[Odger]] [[Coordinates|-373, 207, -810]]. [[Odger]] rejects it due to its quality, and tells the player to fish up another [[Trophy Fish|Volcanic Stonefish]]. He will accept the new fish and give the player a [[Framed Volcanic Stonefish]] to return to Odexar, as well as [[Factions|+100 ቾ Mage Reputation]] and 80 [[Coins]].
The finals series of quests require the player to kill several minibosses and bring their drops to the Dean correspondingly. The drops are:
- [[Hallowed Skull]] from [[Bladesoul]]
- [[Lumino Fiber]] from [[Ashfang]]
- [[Leather Cloth]] from [[Barbarian Duke X]]
Next, the player has to talk to Captain Ahone who tracks down their lost Sulphur to the [[Forgotten Skull]] on the far back side of the island. The player has to defeat [[Kuudra]] in order to finish the questline and receive another [[Factions|+200 ቾ Mage Reputation]].
Afterwards, talk with the [[Dean]] in the cathedral to complete the storyline.
## Barbarians
Upon choosing the Barbarian faction, Elle leads the player out to the [[Burning Desert]] in search of [[Sulphur]], believing it will win them favor with the Piglins. A corrupted [[Flaming Spider]] spawns, which the player must kill. Soon after, Elle discovers an injured Piglin reverted to pig form; curing it with the [[Sulphur]] dropped by the spider reveals their identity as [[Ugo]], son of the Barbarian chief. Both NPCs must be escorted back to [[Dragontail]] by clearing monsters from their path. Speaking to [[Chief Scorn]] earns the player entry into the Barbarian faction.
Chief Scorn reprimands Ugo for not completing his mission: infiltrating the Mage Faction to steal a war machine they are allegedly building. Elle and the player travel to the [[Cathedral]] in his place. The player must sneak past the guards to the [[Dean]]'s office, where the Dean's research can be retrieved from under the table. The research is delivered to the chief, granting [[Factions|+100 ⚒️ Barbarian Reputation]], who again reprimands Ugo for neglecting his training. Once again, the player is sent in his place. First, they must travel to the [[Dojo]]. Speaking to [[Master Tao]] initiates three consecutive dojo challenges; Force, Stamina, and Mastery, which they may complete with any ranking D rank or higher (earning higher ranks of belts). Next, they must defeat a [[Mage Outlaw]], followed by a [[Magma Boss]].
After completing Ugo's training, the player gains [[Factions|+150 ⚒️ Barbarian Reputation]]. Afterwards, they must run errands for a couple of other [[Dragontail]] residents. This includes obtaining 1 [[Trophy Fish|Skeleton Fish]] and 1 [[Trophy Fish|Flyfish]] for [[Lasea]], granting [[Factions|+100 ⚒️ Barbarian Reputation]], and 1 [[Heavy Pearl]] for [[Gris]], granting 200,000 [[Coins]] and [[Factions|+150 ⚒️ Barbarian Reputation]]. (Note: upon activating his quest, Gris will move to the basement of his house when you return).
Eventually, the player is summoned by Chief Scorn, who directs them, Elle, and Ugo to a trail of stolen [[Sulphur]] leading to the [[Forgotten Skull]]. There, Ugo and Captain Ahone confront each other, before realizing that a common enemy is stealing sulphur from both of them. The player has to defeat [[Kuudra]] in order to gain [[Factions|+200 ⚒️ Barbarian Reputation]], and then speak to [[Chief Scorn]] to finish to questline, also granting [[Factions|+200 ⚒️ Barbarian Reputation]].
Linked from: [[Alwin]], [[An]], [[Apprentice (Crimson Isle)]], [[Arba]], [[Arbadak]], [[Baar]], [[Barbarian Emissary]], [[Blaze (Elle's Quest)]], [[Bruuh]], [[Burning Desert]], [[Burning Eye]], [[Captain Ahone]], [[Cathedral]], [[Chak]], [[Chief Scorn]], [[Chief's Hut]], [[Chihai]], [[Chuk]], [[Colore]], [[Community Center (Scarleton)]], [[Counsellor]], [[Crag]], [[Crimson Isle]], [[Dean]], [[Dragontail]], [[Elle]], [[Etc]], [[Ezekiel]], [[Forgotten Skull]], [[Framed Volcanic Stonefish]], [[Grelius]], [[Gris]], [[Grog]], [[Hallowed Skull]], [[Igor]], [[Igrupan]], [[Igrupan's Chicken Coop]], [[Jine]], [[Kaus]], [[Kheharad]] (+40 more)

# Critter Safari/Bells
- [[Coordinates|-68, 66, -43]]: A Bell is hidden on the bottom of an ice platform near the entrance to the Icy biome. Jumping on the platform repeatedly will cause it to flip over, yielding the [[Achievements|What the flip?!]] secret achievement. The bell will be too cold to ring, requiring an [[Icebreaker]] to break it.
- [[Coordinates|-96, 46, -57]]: A Bell is hidden behind a bed found in an underground cavern in the underwater lake in the Icy biome.
- [[Coordinates|-4, 96, -42]]: A Bell is hidden on the roof of the mansion in the Haunted biome. It can be accessed from parkour from the bottom of the mansion, or from climbing to the top of the Icy biome and jumping into the roof.
- [[Coordinates|47, 55, -7]]: A Bell is hidden at the edge of the Critter Safari, accessed from the Forest biome waterfalls, before dropping down and walking towards the Haunted biome while on the outer rim of the Critter Safari. The ledge can be exited safely by ascending from the vine at [[Coordinates|16, 53, 79]].
- [[Coordinates|-30, 125, 59]]: A Bell is hidden near the top of the Forest biome, requiring parkour across a large tree to a floating island.
- [[Coordinates|-90, 109, 16]]: A Bell is hidden at the top of the Cavern biome: you will need to climb from a fake cactus onto a large stack from [[Coordinates|-138, 102, 26]], parkour across a tree, and make it to another stack where the bell is found.
- [[Coordinates|-50, 81, 0]]: A Bell is hidden at the top of the landing zone, accessible from an ice spike by the entrance to the Icy biome.
Each bell corresponds to a specific color in the rainbow. Completing the rainbow and returning to the landing zone yields the following dialogue from [["Hunter" Tobias]]:
[NPC] "Hunter" Tobias: Uh guys, you better come and see this...
Finally, a cutscene plays as the player walks into the landing zone. As the player looks at the rainbow, the following dialogue plays:
Wow, a rainbow. I haven't seen one of them in a long time. Isn't it beautiful? You know, there are so many memories in a rainbow. I remember seeing one while playing with all my friends, back when we were very small. The next time I saw one was many years later, after one of them died. Sometimes it feels like, every time I see one, there are less of my kind left to see it with me. But it's still oddly comforting. Before I was here, there were rainbows. After I am gone, there will still be rainbows. Throughout my short life, everything I once knew has changed beyond recognition. As for the rainbows, I don't think they notice the difference.
With each dialogue, the player's [[Attributes|Miracle Chance]] attribute gains +1 level, maxing out upon the cutscene ending. Players also receive [[Achievements|The Miracle in a sunbeam]] secret achievement.
During this cutscene, a custom [[Hypixel SkyBlock OST|song]] plays:

# Crop Analyzer
Aliases: Analysis, Analyze, Analyzer, Crop Analyzer Milestone, DNA Analysis Milestone
[Crop Analyzer]
The Crop Analyzer is a machine in the [[Underground Lab]] that allows players to analyze [[Mutations]] unlocked after completing [[Beth]]'s quest.
## DNA Sequencing
Upon analyzing a Mutation, a minigame in which one must sequence its DNA begins, shown by 4 colored nodes on both sides of the interface and several connecting strings in the middle. Clicking on a string and then clicking on another one in the same row swaps the positions around. The player must connect each colored node to the other colored node on the opposite side through sequencing the strings properly.
Completing the minigame grants [[Copper]]. Additionally, analyzing a mutation for the first time grants some bonus [[Copper]] and progress towards Milestones.
|Slot|Mutation|First Time [[Copper]]|[[Copper]]|Analysis Cost|Coins per Copper|

## Gallery
- Crop Analyzer and Jake in the Underground Lab Crop Analyzer and [[Jake]] in the [[Underground Lab]]
## Trivia
- The analysis [[Coins|Coin]] cost of [[Mutations]] is 2,000× higher than the given [[Copper]], with the exception of [[Cheesebite]] where the analysis cost is 1,000× higher instead.
- Mass analysing [[Mutations]] grants 1 [[Copper]] more per [[Mutations|Mutation]] than manual analysis.

# Deployables
Aliases: Deployable, Flux
[Deployables]
Deployables are a class of items that can be placed down to provide a buff for players.
## Mechanics
Deployables are items that can be placed down to provide a buff for players in their radius. Only one Deployable can be placed down at a time.
### Upgrades
|Slot|Upgrade|Effect|Notes|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Mana Disintegrator]]|[[Mana Disintegrator]]|Can be combined in an [[Anvil]] with a Deployable to reduce their [[Mana]] cost by 1%.|Can be applied up to 10 times.; When applied to [[Totem of Corruption]], the tooltip won't reflect the mana cost reduction, despite it applying.; Can be applied to Black Holes despite having no effect.|
|[[Jalapeno Book]]|[[Jalapeno Book]]|Can be combined in an [[Anvil]] with a Deployable to gain a small but permanent stat boost of +5 [[Crit Damage]] and +1% [[Crit Chance]].|Can only be applied once.; Cannot be applied to Black Holes and Lanterns|
|[[Power Scrolls]]|[[Power Scrolls]]|Can be combined in an [[Anvil]] with a Deployable to gain a small bonus, depending on the scroll, whenever the item's ability is used.|Can only be applied once.; Only applicable to Flares and [[Totem of Corruption]].|

|Slot|Name|Rarity|Effect|Source|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Radiant Power Orb]]|[[Radiant Power Orb]]|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|Item Ability: Deploy; Place an orb for 30s buffing up to 5 players within 18 blocks.; Costs 50% of max mana; Costs 50 [[Vitality]]; Only one deployable buff applies; Orb Buff: Radiant; Heal yourself for 30 [[Health]] per second.; Heal others for 15 [[Health]] per second.; Gain +25 [[Health Regen]].|[[Crafting]]; Wolf Slayer II (2) ([[Wolf Slayer]]) Recipe: [[Wolf Tooth]] x2 ×8 + [[Enchanted Emerald]] x8 → [[Radiant Power Orb]]|
|[[Mana Flux Power Orb]]|[[Mana Flux Power Orb]]|RARE ([[Rarity]])|Item Ability: Deploy; Place an orb for 30s buffing up to 5 players within 18 blocks.; Costs 50% of max mana; Costs 50 [[Vitality]]; Only one deployable buff applies; Orb Buff: Mana Flux; Heal yourself for 40 [[Health]] per second.; Heal others for 20 [[Health]] per second.; Gain +50 [[Health Regen]].; Grants +50% base mana regen.; Grants +10 [[Strength]].|[[Crafting]]; Wolf Slayer VI (6) ([[Wolf Slayer]]) Recipe: [[Enchanted Lapis Lazuli Block]] ×4 + [[Golden Tooth]] x8 ×4 + [[Radiant Power Orb]] → [[Mana Flux Power Orb]]|
|[[Overflux Power Orb]]|[[Overflux Power Orb]]|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|Item Ability: Deploy; Place an orb for 60s buffing up to 5 players within 18 blocks.; Costs 50% of max mana; Costs 50 [[Vitality]]; Only one deployable buff applies; Orb Buff: Overflux; Heal yourself for 50 [[Health]] per second.; Heal others for 25 [[Health]] per second.; Gain +100 [[Health Regen]].; Grants +100% base mana regen.; Grants +25 [[Strength]].|[[Crafting]]; Wolf Slayer VII (7) ([[Wolf Slayer]]) Recipe: [[Golden Tooth]] x32 ×4 + [[Overflux Capacitor]] + [[Enchanted Redstone Block]] x8 ×3 + [[Mana Flux Power Orb]] → [[Overflux Power Orb]]|
|[[Plasmaflux Power Orb]]|[[Plasmaflux Power Orb]]|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|Item Ability: Deploy; Place an orb for 60s buffing up to 5 players within 20 blocks.; Costs 50% of max mana; Costs 50 [[Vitality]]; Only one deployable buff applies; Orb Buff: Plasmaflux; Heal yourself for 60 [[Health]] per second.; Heal others for 30 [[Health]] per second.; Gain +125 [[Health Regen]].; Grants +125% base mana regen.; Grants +35 [[Strength]].|[[Crafting]]; Wolf Slayer VIII (8) ([[Wolf Slayer]]) Recipe: [[Enchanted Obsidian]] x64 ×4 + [[Plasma Nucleus]] + [[Golden Tooth]] x24 ×3 + [[Overflux Power Orb]] → [[Plasmaflux Power Orb]]|

|[[Warning Flare]]|[[Warning Flare]]|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|Item Ability: Deploy; Shoot the flare up in the sky for 3m buffing up to 8 players within 40 blocks.; Mana Cost: 300 [[Mana]]; Vitality Cost: 10; Cooldown: 20s; Flare Buff: Warning Flare; Gain +10 [[Vitality]].; Gain +10 [[True Defense]].; Heal 30 [[Health]] per second while on the Crimson Isle and Kuudra.; Only one deployable buff applies.|[[Crafting]]; Blaze Slayer III (3) ([[Blaze Slayer]]) Recipe: [[Enchanted Sulphur]] x16 + [[Molten Powder]] x8 ×2 → [[Warning Flare]]|
|[[Alert Flare]]|[[Alert Flare]]|RARE ([[Rarity]])|Item Ability: Deploy; Shoot the flare up in the sky for 3m buffing up to 8 players within 40 blocks.; Vitality Cost: 10; Mana Cost: 300 [[Mana]]; Cooldown: 20s; Flare Buff: Alert Flare; Grants +50% base mana regen.; Gain +15 [[Vitality]].; Gain +20 [[True Defense]].; Heal 40 [[Health]] per second while on the Crimson Isle and Kuudra.; Gain +10 [[Ferocity]].; Only one deployable buff applies.|[[Crafting]]; Blaze Slayer VI (6) ([[Blaze Slayer]]) Recipe: [[Enchanted Sulphur]] x32 + [[Inferno Vertex]] x8 ×2 + [[Molten Powder]] x64 + [[Warning Flare]] → [[Alert Flare]]|

# Enigma Souls
Aliases: Enigma Soul
[Enigma Souls]
Enigma Souls are collectible orbs scattered throughout the [[Rift Dimension]] and can be traded with [[Enigma]].
## Mechanics
Enigma Souls are objects that take the form of placed orbs and are scattered and hidden around parts of the [[Rift Dimension|Rift]].
Players can interact with Enigma Souls by right-clicking them. Each soul is unique and can be collected only once. A message in the chat is displayed to the player each time they interact with a soul informing them that they have collected the soul or had previously discovered it.
Enigma Souls may be exchanged in groups of four with [[Enigma]] in [[Enigma's Crib]] in exchange for [[Motes]]. The player is rewarded with +2 [[Speed]] and +4 [[Mana Regen]] on their [[Enigma Cloak]] each upgrade.
## Locations
Enigma Souls can be obtained and found in many different ways throughout the [[Rift Dimension]].
Note: Click on the rows to keep track of the Enigma Souls collected. This persists even after reloading or refreshing the page.:
